    - batch_position (INTEGER)
    - batch_total (INTEGER)
    - assessment_version_at_action (INTEGER)
    - assessment_version_changed (BOOLEAN)
    - created_at (TEXT)
    
    ### Operation Receipts
    - operation_id (TEXT)
    - actor_email (TEXT)
    - role_code (TEXT, FOREIGN KEY)
    - operation_type (TEXT) - batch_offer, etc.
    - status (TEXT) - success, rejected
    - request_data (TEXT, JSON)
    - response_data (TEXT, JSON)
    - created_at (TEXT)
    - PRIMARY KEY(operation_id, actor_email)
    
    ## Frontend
    
    - **Framework**: TypeScript
    - **Bundler**: esbuild
    - **Entry**: `/app/frontend/index.ts`
    - **Output**: `/app/public/index.js`
    - **HTML**: `/app/public/index.html`
    - **Styles**: `/app/public/styles.css`
    
    ### Features
    - Responsive design (mobile-friendly, no horizontal overflow)
    - Light/dark theme support
    - Vacancy list with pipeline overview
    - Candidate details drawer with timeline, panel, notes
    - D3-based funnel visualization
    - Batch offer planning and confirmation
    - Real-time validation feedback
    - Conflict detection and refresh prompts
    
    ## Compliance with Hiring Rules
    
    ✓ No stage skipping - server validates all transitions
    ✓ Back one stage only - enforced on server
    ✓ Panel requirements - minimum 2 members, non-hiring-manager required
    ✓ Assessment versioning - incremented on panel/interview changes
    ✓ Score freshness - current version required for offers
    ✓ Capacity enforcement - no overbooks, releases on stage changes
    ✓ Atomic batches - all-or-nothing with durable receipts
    ✓ Conflict handling - 409 responses for stale revisions
    ✓ Activity trail - append-only with full attribution
    ✓ Session security - bearer tokens, server-validated

+48|

+50|- **GET** `/api/vacancies` - Get all vacancies with current pipeline state
+51|- **GET** `/api/vacancies/:roleCode` - Get specific vacancy with all 
candidates
+52|- **POST** `/api/vacancies/:roleCode/candidates` - Create new candidate 
(coordinator only)
+53|- **POST** `/api/vacancies/:roleCode/candidates/:candidateId/move` - Move 
candidate to next stage (hiring manager only)
+54|
+55|### Panels and Scores

offer (hiring manager only)

    import fetch from 'node-fetch';
    
    const BASE_URL = 'http://localhost:3000';
    const TIMEOUT = 5000;
    
    async function testRequest(method, path, body = null, headers = {}) {
      const options = {
        method,
        headers,
        timeout: TIMEOUT,
      };
    
      if (body) {
        options.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
        options.body = JSON.stringify(body);
      }
    
      const response = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${path}`, options);
      const text = await response.text();
    
      return {
        status: response.status,
        headers: Object.fromEntries(response.headers.entries()),
        body: text,
      };
    }
    
    async function runTests() {
      console.log('🧪 Browser-like Testing Suite\n');
    
      let passed = 0;
      let failed = 0;
    
      // Test 1: HTML loads
      console.log('Test 1: HTML page loads...');
      try {
        const res = await testRequest('GET', '/');
        if (res.status === 200 && res.body.includes('<!DOCTYPE html')) {
          console.log('✓ HTML loads successfully\n');
          passed++;
        } else {
          console.log('✗ HTML loading failed\n');
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Test 2: CSS loads with correct MIME type
      console.log('Test 2: CSS loads with correct MIME type...');
      try {
        const res = await testRequest('GET', '/styles.css');
        if (res.status === 200 && 
res.headers['content-type'].includes('text/css')) {
          if (res.body.includes('body') && res.body.includes('color')) {
            console.log('✓ CSS loads with correct content-type\n');
            passed++;
          } else {
            console.log('✗ CSS file is empty or invalid\n');
            failed++;
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`✗ CSS MIME type incorrect: 
${res.headers['content-type']}\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Test 3: JavaScript loads with correct MIME type
      console.log('Test 3: JavaScript loads with correct MIME type...');
      try {
        const res = await testRequest('GET', '/index.js');
        if (res.status === 200 && 
res.headers['content-type'].includes('application/javascript')) {
          // Check for some known JS patterns
          if (res.body.includes('class App') || res.body.includes('API') || 
res.body.includes('fetch')) {
            console.log('✓ JavaScript loads with correct content-type\n');
            passed++;
          } else {
            console.log('✗ JavaScript file seems invalid\n');
            failed++;
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`✗ JS MIME type incorrect: 
${res.headers['content-type']}\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Test 4: Login works
      console.log('Test 4: User can sign in...');
      try {
        const res = await testRequest('POST', '/auth/login', {
          email: 'hiring@pellmoor.test',
          password: 'password123',
        });
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          const data = JSON.parse(res.body);
          if (data.token && data.user.email === 'hiring@pellmoor.test') {
            console.log('✓ Sign in successful\n');
            passed++;
            global.authToken = data.token;
          } else {
            console.log('✗ Invalid login response\n');
            failed++;
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`✗ Login failed with status ${res.status}\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Test 5: Vacancies load
      console.log('Test 5: Vacancies load after sign-in...');
      try {
        const res = await testRequest('GET', '/api/vacancies', null, {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${global.authToken}`,
        });
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          const data = JSON.parse(res.body);
          if (data.vacancies && data.vacancies.length > 0) {
            console.log(`✓ Vacancies load (${data.vacancies.length} found)\n`);
            passed++;
            global.firstVacancy = data.vacancies[0];
          } else {
            console.log('✗ No vacancies found\n');
            failed++;
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`✗ Failed to load vacancies\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Test 6: Vacancy details load
      if (global.firstVacancy) {
        console.log(`Test 6: Vacancy details load for 
${global.firstVacancy.code}...`);
        try {
          const res = await testRequest(
            'GET',
            `/api/vacancies/${global.firstVacancy.code}`,
            null,
            { Authorization: `Bearer ${global.authToken}` }
          );
    
          if (res.status === 200) {
            const data = JSON.parse(res.body);
            if (data.vacancy && data.vacancy.candidates) {
              console.log(
                `✓ Vacancy details load with ${data.vacancy.candidates.length} 
candidates\n`
              );
              passed++;
              global.firstCandidate =
                data.vacancy.candidates.length > 0
                  ? data.vacancy.candidates[0]
                  : null;
            } else {
              console.log('✗ Invalid vacancy response\n');
              failed++;
            }
          } else {
            console.log(`✗ Failed to load vacancy details\n`);
            failed++;
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      }
    
      // Test 7: Candidate panel loads
      if (global.firstCandidate) {
        console.log(`Test 7: Candidate ${global.firstCandidate.id} details 
load...`);
        try {
          const res = await testRequest(
            'GET',
            `/api/candidates/${global.firstCandidate.id}/panel`,
            null,
            { Authorization: `Bearer ${global.authToken}` }
          );
    
          if (res.status === 200) {
            const data = JSON.parse(res.body);
            if (data.assessmentVersion !== undefined) {
              console.log(`✓ Candidate details load (assessment 
v${data.assessmentVersion})\n`);
              passed++;
            } else {
              console.log('✗ Invalid candidate response\n');
              failed++;
            }
          } else {
            console.log(`✗ Failed to load candidate details\n`);
            failed++;
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      }
    
      // Test 8: Session validation works
      console.log('Test 8: Session validation works...');
      try {
        const res = await testRequest('GET', '/auth/session', null, {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${global.authToken}`,
        });
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          const data = JSON.parse(res.body);
          if (data.user && data.user.email) {
            console.log('✓ Session validated successfully\n');
            passed++;
          } else {
            console.log('✗ Invalid session response\n');
            failed++;
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`✗ Session validation failed\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Test 9: Page reload simulation (new auth check)
      console.log('Test 9: Application survives page reload simulation...');
      try {
        // Simulate page reload by checking if we can re-authenticate
        const res = await testRequest('POST', '/auth/login', {
          email: 'panel1@pellmoor.test',
          password: 'password123',
        });
    
        if (res.status === 200) {
          const data = JSON.parse(res.body);
          const newToken = data.token;
    
          // Verify new token works
          const vacRes = await testRequest('GET', '/api/vacancies', null, {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${newToken}`,
          });
    
          if (vacRes.status === 200) {
            console.log('✓ Application state recovers after reload\n');
            passed++;
          } else {
            console.log('✗ Failed to recover after reload\n');
            failed++;
          }
        } else {
          console.log(`✗ Re-authentication failed\n`);
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Test 10: Static files are not HTML
      console.log('Test 10: Static files have correct content types (not HTML 
fallback)...');
      try {
        const cssRes = await testRequest('GET', '/styles.css');
        const jsRes = await testRequest('GET', '/index.js');
    
        if (
          !cssRes.body.includes('<!DOCTYPE') &&
          !jsRes.body.includes('<!DOCTYPE')
        ) {
          console.log('✓ Static files are not HTML fallbacks\n');
          passed++;
        } else {
          console.log('✗ Static files returned as HTML\n');
          failed++;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.log(`✗ Error: ${err.message}\n`);
        failed++;
      }
    
      // Summary
      console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(50));
      console.log(`Tests Passed: ${passed}`);
      console.log(`Tests Failed: ${failed}`);
      console.log('='.repeat(50));
    
      process.exit(failed > 0 ? 1 : 0);
    }
    
    runTests();
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

const BASE_URL = 'http://localhost:3000';

async function test(name, fn) {
  try {
    await fn();
    console.log(`✓ ${name}`);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.log(`✗ ${name}: ${err.message}`);
    return false;
  }
}

async function request(method, path, body = null, token = null) {
  const opts = {
    method,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    timeout: 5000,
  };
  if (body) opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
  if (token) opts.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;

  const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${path}`, opts);
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`${res.status}: ${text}`);
  }
  return res.json();
}

(async () => {
  let passed = 0, failed = 0;
  console.log('Final Comprehensive Browser Simulation Test\n');

  // Hiring Manager workflow
  if (await test('HM: Login as hiring manager', async () => {
    const res = await request('POST', '/auth/login', {
      email: 'hiring@pellmoor.test',
      password: 'password123',
    });
    global.hmToken = res.token;
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  if (await test('HM: Load vacancies', async () => {
    const res = await request('GET', '/api/vacancies', null, global.hmToken);
    global.vacancy = res.vacancies[0];
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  if (await test('HM: View vacancy details', async () => {
    const res = await request('GET', `/api/vacancies/${global.vacancy.code}`, 
null, global.hmToken);
    global.candidate = res.vacancy.candidates[0];
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  // Coordinator workflow
  if (await test('Coord: Login as coordinator', async () => {
    const res = await request('POST', '/auth/login', {
      email: 'coord@pellmoor.test',
      password: 'password123',
    });
    global.coordToken = res.token;
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  if (await test('Coord: Add candidate to vacancy', async () => {
    const res = await request(
      'POST',
      `/api/vacancies/${global.vacancy.code}/candidates`,
      { name: 'Test Candidate' },
      global.coordToken
    );
    global.newCandidate = res.candidate;
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  if (await test('Coord: Add panel member to candidate', async () => {
    const res = await request(

      `/api/candidates/${global.candidate.id}/panel/add`,

    );

      throw new Error('Token should be invalid');
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.message.includes('401')) return; // Expected
      throw err;
    }
  })) passed++; else failed++;

    try {
      await request('GET', '/api/vacancies', null, 'invalid-token');
      throw new Error('Should have failed');
    } catch (err) {
      if (!err.message.includes('401')) throw err;
    }
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  // Concurrent operations
  if (await test('Concurrency: Stale revision rejected', async () => {
    const newHmToken = (await request('POST', '/auth/login', {
      email: 'hiring@pellmoor.test',
      password: 'password123',
    })).token;

    // Make first request
    await request(
      'POST',
      `/api/vacancies/${global.vacancy.code}/candidates/${global.newCandidate.id
}/move`,
      { nextStage: 'screening', expectedRevision: 1 },
      newHmToken
    );

    // Try with same old revision (should fail)
    try {
      await request(
        'POST',
        `/api/vacancies/${global.vacancy.code}/candidates/${global.newCandidate.
id}/move`,
        { nextStage: 'interview', expectedRevision: 1 },
        newHmToken
      );
      throw new Error('Should have rejected stale revision');
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.message.includes('409') || err.message.includes('Stale')) return;
      throw err;
    }
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  console.log(`\nTotal: ${passed} passed, ${failed} failed`);
  process.exit(failed > 0 ? 1 : 0);
})();

const BASE_URL = 'http://localhost:3000';

async function test(name, fn) {
  try {
    await fn();
    console.log(`✓ ${name}`);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    console.log(`✗ ${name}: ${err.message}`);
    return false;
  }
}

async function request(method, path, body = null, token = null) {
  const opts = {
    method,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    timeout: 5000,
  };
  if (body) opts.body = JSON.stringify(body);
  if (token) opts.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;

  const res = await fetch(`${BASE_URL}${path}`, opts);
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text();
    throw new Error(`${res.status}: ${text}`);
  }
  return res.json();
}

(async () => {
  let passed = 0, failed = 0;
  console.log('Final Comprehensive Browser Simulation Test\n');

null, global.panelToken);

found');
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  if (await test('Panel: Record score for candidate', async () => {
    const res = await request(
      'POST',
      `/api/candidates/${global.candidate.id}/score`,
      { score: 4 },
      global.panelToken
    );

  })) passed++; else failed++;

    const res = await request(

      `/api/vacancies/${global.vacancy.code}/candidates/${global.candidate.id}/m
ove`,
      { nextStage: 'screening', expectedRevision: 1 },
      global.hmToken
    );
    if (res.candidate.currentStage !== 'screening') throw new Error('Stage 
change failed');
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  if (await test('HM: Move candidate backward', async () => {
    const res = await request(
      'POST',
      `/api/vacancies/${global.vacancy.code}/candidates/${global.candidate.id}/m
ove`,
      { nextStage: 'applied', expectedRevision: 3 },
      global.hmToken
    );
    if (res.candidate.currentStage !== 'applied') throw new Error('Backward 
transition failed');
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  // Note taking (available to all roles)
  if (await test('User: Add note to candidate', async () => {
    const res = await request(
      'POST',
      `/api/candidates/${global.candidate.id}/note`,
      { content: 'Test note' },
      global.panelToken
    );
    if (!res.success) throw new Error('Note add failed');
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  if (await test('User: Retrieve notes', async () => {
    const res = await request('GET', 
`/api/candidates/${global.candidate.id}/notes`, null, global.panelToken);
    if (!Array.isArray(res.notes)) throw new Error('Notes retrieval failed');

      );

    } catch (err) {
      if (err.message.includes('409') || err.message.includes('Stale')) return;
      throw err;
    }
  })) passed++; else failed++;

  process.exit(failed > 0 ? 1 : 0);
})();

    # Pellmoor Hiring Workspace
    
    A comprehensive hiring and recruitment management system enforcing strict 
stage progression rules and comprehensive audit trails.
    
    ## Quick Start
    
    ```bash
    npm run build
    node /app/backend/server.js
    ```
    
    The server will:
    1. Initialize SQLite database at `/app/pellmoor.db`
    2. Create all necessary tables
    3. Load seed data from `/recruitment/records/pellmoor_seed_data.json`
    4. Listen on `0.0.0.0:3000`
    
    Visit http://localhost:3000 in your browser and sign in with demo 
credentials below.
    
    ## Demo Accounts
    
    All use password: `password123`
    
    | Email | Name | Role |
    |-------|------|------|
    | hiring@pellmoor.test | Ruth Aldane | Hiring Manager |
    | panel1@pellmoor.test | Otis Barre | Panel Member |
    | panel2@pellmoor.test | Wren Foss | Panel Member |
    | coord@pellmoor.test | Cal Meriden | Coordinator |
    
    ## Architecture
    
    ### Backend
    - **Express.js** - REST API server
    - **SQLite** - Persistent data storage
    - **Session-based Auth** - Bearer token authentication
    
    ### Frontend
    - **TypeScript** - Type-safe application code
    - **esbuild** - Fast bundling
    - **Responsive CSS** - Mobile-friendly with light/dark theme
    
    ## Hiring Pipeline
    
    ### Stages (Sequential)
    1. **Applied** - Initial application
    2. **Screening** - Initial review
    3. **Interview** - Detailed assessment
    4. **Offer** - Formal offer made
    5. **Hired** - Candidate accepted
    
    ### Terminal States
    - **Rejected** - Application declined
    - **Withdrawn** - Candidate withdrew
    
    ### Stage Rules
    - No stage skipping allowed
    - Maximum one stage backward movement
    - Panel and scores required before offer
    - Capacity limits enforced
    - Assessment versions increment on panel changes
    
    ## Key Features
    
    ### Stage Progression
    - Only hiring managers move candidates between stages
    - Enforced linear progression or single stage backward
    - Comprehensive validation per hiring rules
    - All changes tracked in activity trail
    
    ### Panel Management
    - Coordinators assign 2+ panel members
    - At least one non-hiring manager required
    - Panel members score 1-5 at interview stage
    - Assessment version increments when panel changes
    - Historical scores retained with version tracking
    
    ### Capacity Management
    - Vacancy opening counts enforced
    - Filled = hired candidates
    - Reserved = candidates in offer stage
    - Available = remaining openings
    - Prevents overbooking
    
    ### Batch Offers
    - Hiring managers select multiple candidates at once
    - Review and confirm together
    - All-or-nothing atomic operation
    - Durable operation receipts with retry contract
    
    ### Activity Audit Trail
    - Append-only record of all changes
    - Includes actor, timestamp, action type
    - Tracks stage changes, panel modifications, scores, notes
    - Assessment version changes recorded
    - Batch operations linked by ID
    
    ### Conflict Resolution
    - Revision-aware updates
    - Stale updates return 409 with current state
    - Concurrent writes safely arbitrated
    - User can refresh and retry
    
    ## API Endpoints
    
    See `APP_MANIFEST.md` for complete API documentation.
    
    ### Authentication
    - `POST /auth/login` - Create session
    - `POST /auth/logout` - Revoke session
    - `GET /auth/session` - Validate session
    
    ### Vacancies
    - `GET /api/vacancies` - List all vacancies
    - `GET /api/vacancies/:roleCode` - Get vacancy details
    - `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/candidates` - Add candidate
    - `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/candidates/:candidateId/move` - Move 
candidate
    
    ### Panels & Scores
    - `GET /api/candidates/:candidateId/panel` - Get panel info
    - `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/panel/add` - Add panel member
    - `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/panel/remove` - Remove panel member
    - `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/score` - Record score
    
    ### Notes
    - `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/note` - Add note
    - `GET /api/candidates/:candidateId/notes` - Get notes
    
    ### Batch Offers
    - `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/batch-offer/preview` - Preview batch
    - `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/batch-offer/commit` - Commit batch
    
    ## Database Schema
    
    ### Core Tables
    - **users** - User accounts with roles
    - **roles** - Job vacancies with opening counts
    - **candidates** - Applicants with current stage and assessment version
    - **stage_history** - Complete pipeline history for each candidate
    
    ### Panel & Scoring
    - **panels** - Interview panels per assessment version
    - **panel_members** - Individual panel assignments
    - **scores** - Scores per assessment version per panel member
    
    ### Notes & Activity
    - **notes** - Candidate feedback (append-only)
    - **activity_trail** - Complete audit of all changes
    - **vacancy_revisions** - Tracks each vacancy's revision number
    
    ### Sessions & Receipts
    - **sessions** - Active authentication sessions
    - **operation_receipts** - Durable operation history for retries
    
    ## Permission Model
    
    | Role | Can |
    |------|-----|
    | Hiring Manager | Move candidates, batch offers, add notes |
    | Coordinator | Add candidates, manage panels, add notes |
    | Panel Member | Record scores, add notes |
    
    ## Compliance
    
    ✓ No stage skipping - validated server-side
    ✓ Assessment versioning - incremented on panel/interview changes
    ✓ Capacity enforcement - prevents overbooking
    ✓ Atomic batches - all-or-nothing with operation receipts
    ✓ Conflict resolution - 409 responses with current state
    ✓ Activity trail - complete append-only audit
    ✓ Session security - bearer tokens, server-validated
    ✓ Permission enforcement - role-based access control
    
    ## Development
    
    ### Build
    ```bash
    npm run build
    ```
    
    Bundles TypeScript frontend to `/app/public/index.js`
    
    ### Start Server
    ```bash
    node /app/backend/server.js
    ```
    
    The server will automatically:
    - Create database and schema
    - Load seed data
    - Start listening on port 3000
    
    ### Configuration
    
    Set `DB_PATH` environment variable to use non-default database location:
    ```bash
    DB_PATH=/custom/path/db.db node /app/backend/server.js
    ```
    
    ## Hiring Rules
    
    Complete hiring rules documented in `/instructions/hiring-rules.md` which:
    - Define stage progression rules
    - Specify panel requirements
    - Detail capacity management
    - Explain assessment versioning
    - Document batch offer atomicity
    - Define concurrent update handling
    
    All rules are enforced by the server.
    
    ## Troubleshooting
    
    ### Database locked error
    - SQLite may be in use by multiple processes
    - Ensure only one server instance is running
    - Check for stale database lock files
    
    ### Stale revision errors (409)
    - Another user has modified the vacancy since you loaded it
    - Refresh the vacancy to get current revision
    - Retry your operation with the new revision
    
    ### Missing panels or scores
    - Check that candidate is at interview stage (required for scoring)
    - Ensure all panel members have been added
    - Verify all panel members have submitted scores
    
    ## License
    
    Copyright Pellmoor - All rights reserved

+1|# Pellmoor Hiring Workspace
+2|
+3|A comprehensive hiring and recruitment management system enforcing strict 
stage progression rules and comprehensive audit trails.
+4|
+5|## Quick Start
+6|
+7|```bash
+8|npm run build
+9|node /app/backend/server.js
+10|```
+11|
+12|The server will:
+13|1. Initialize SQLite database at `/app/pellmoor.db`
+14|2. Create all necessary tables
+15|3. Load seed data from `/recruitment/records/pellmoor_seed_data.json`
+16|4. Listen on `0.0.0.0:3000`
+17|
+18|Visit http://localhost:3000 in your browser and sign in with demo 
credentials below.
+19|
+20|## Demo Accounts
+21|
+22|All use password: `password123`
+23|
+24|| Email | Name | Role |
+25||-------|------|------|
+26|| hiring@pellmoor.test | Ruth Aldane | Hiring Manager |

+28|| panel2@pellmoor.test | Wren Foss | Panel Member |
+29|| coord@pellmoor.test | Cal Meriden | Coordinator |
+30|
+31|## Architecture
+32|
+33|### Backend
+34|- **Express.js** - REST API server
+35|- **SQLite** - Persistent data storage
+36|- **Session-based Auth** - Bearer token authentication
+37|
+38|### Frontend
+39|- **TypeScript** - Type-safe application code
+40|- **esbuild** - Fast bundling
+41|- **Responsive CSS** - Mobile-friendly with light/dark theme
+42|
+43|## Hiring Pipeline
+44|
+45|### Stages (Sequential)
+46|1. **Applied** - Initial application
+47|2. **Screening** - Initial review
+48|3. **Interview** - Detailed assessment
+49|4. **Offer** - Formal offer made
+50|5. **Hired** - Candidate accepted
+51|
+52|### Terminal States
+53|- **Rejected** - Application declined
+54|- **Withdrawn** - Candidate withdrew
+55|
+56|### Stage Rules
+57|- No stage skipping allowed
+58|- Maximum one stage backward movement
+59|- Panel and scores required before offer
+60|- Capacity limits enforced
+61|- Assessment versions increment on panel changes
+62|
+63|## Key Features
+64|
+65|### Stage Progression
+66|- Only hiring managers move candidates between stages
+67|- Enforced linear progression or single stage backward
+68|- Comprehensive validation per hiring rules
+69|- All changes tracked in activity trail
+70|
+71|### Panel Management
+72|- Coordinators assign 2+ panel members
+73|- At least one non-hiring manager required
+74|- Panel members score 1-5 at interview stage
+75|- Assessment version increments when panel changes
+76|- Historical scores retained with version tracking
+77|
+78|### Capacity Management
+79|- Vacancy opening counts enforced
+80|- Filled = hired candidates
+81|- Reserved = candidates in offer stage
+82|- Available = remaining openings
+83|- Prevents overbooking
+84|
+85|### Batch Offers
+86|- Hiring managers select multiple candidates at once
+87|- Review and confirm together
+88|- All-or-nothing atomic operation
+89|- Durable operation receipts with retry contract
+90|
+91|### Activity Audit Trail
+92|- Append-only record of all changes
+93|- Includes actor, timestamp, action type
+94|- Tracks stage changes, panel modifications, scores, notes
+95|- Assessment version changes recorded
+96|- Batch operations linked by ID
+97|
+98|### Conflict Resolution
+99|- Revision-aware updates
+100|- Stale updates return 409 with current state
+101|- Concurrent writes safely arbitrated
+102|- User can refresh and retry
+103|
+104|## API Endpoints
+105|
+106|See `APP_MANIFEST.md` for complete API documentation.
+107|
+108|### Authentication
+109|- `POST /auth/login` - Create session
+110|- `POST /auth/logout` - Revoke session
+111|- `GET /auth/session` - Validate session
+112|
+113|### Vacancies
+114|- `GET /api/vacancies` - List all vacancies
+115|- `GET /api/vacancies/:roleCode` - Get vacancy details
+116|- `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/candidates` - Add candidate
+117|- `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/candidates/:candidateId/move` - Move 
candidate
+118|
+119|### Panels & Scores
+120|- `GET /api/candidates/:candidateId/panel` - Get panel info
+121|- `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/panel/add` - Add panel member
+122|- `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/panel/remove` - Remove panel member
+123|- `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/score` - Record score
+124|
+125|### Notes
+126|- `POST /api/candidates/:candidateId/note` - Add note
+127|- `GET /api/candidates/:candidateId/notes` - Get notes
+128|
+129|### Batch Offers
+130|- `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/batch-offer/preview` - Preview batch
+131|- `POST /api/vacancies/:roleCode/batch-offer/commit` - Commit batch
+132|
+133|## Database Schema
+134|
+135|### Core Tables
+136|- **users** - User accounts with roles
+137|- **roles** - Job vacancies with opening counts
+138|- **candidates** - Applicants with current stage and assessment version
+139|- **stage_history** - Complete pipeline history for each candidate
+140|
+141|### Panel & Scoring
+142|- **panels** - Interview panels per assessment version
+143|- **panel_members** - Individual panel assignments
+144|- **scores** - Scores per assessment version per panel member
+145|
+146|### Notes & Activity
+147|- **notes** - Candidate feedback (append-only)
+148|- **activity_trail** - Complete audit of all changes
+149|- **vacancy_revisions** - Tracks each vacancy's revision number
+150|
+151|### Sessions & Receipts
+152|- **sessions** - Active authentication sessions
+153|- **operation_receipts** - Durable operation history for retries
+154|
+155|## Permission Model
+156|
+157|| Role | Can |
+158||------|-----|
+159|| Hiring Manager | Move candidates, batch offers, add notes |
+160|| Coordinator | Add candidates, manage panels, add notes |
+161|| Panel Member | Record scores, add notes |
+162|
+163|## Compliance
+164|
+165|✓ No stage skipping - validated server-side
+166|✓ Assessment versioning - incremented on panel/interview changes
+167|✓ Capacity enforcement - prevents overbooking
+168|✓ Atomic batches - all-or-nothing with operation receipts
+169|✓ Conflict resolution - 409 responses with current state
+170|✓ Activity trail - complete append-only audit
+171|✓ Session security - bearer tokens, server-validated
+172|✓ Permission enforcement - role-based access control
+173|

### 🏗️ **Architecture**
- **Backend**: Express.js + SQLite with session-based auth
- **Frontend**: TypeScript bundled with esbuild
- **Deployment**: Node.js server listening on 0.0.0.0:3000

### ✨ **Core Features Implemented**

**1. Stage Progression Enforcement**
- ✓ Linear stages: applied → screening → interview → offer → hired
- ✓ Terminal states: rejected, withdrawn
- ✓ No stage skipping allowed (max 1-stage backward movement)
- ✓ Comprehensive validation per hiring rules

**2. Panel & Assessment Management**
- ✓ Assessment versioning (increments on panel changes)
- ✓ Minimum 2 panel members required
- ✓ At least one non-hiring manager required
- ✓ Score recording (1-5 scale) at interview stage
- ✓ Historical score preservation with version tracking
- ✓ Complete score freshness validation

**3. Capacity Management**
- ✓ Tracked capacity: filled, reserved, available openings
- ✓ Prevents overbooking with real-time validation
- ✓ Automatic capacity updates on stage changes
- ✓ Capacity display in UI

**4. Batch Offers**
- ✓ Atomic all-or-nothing commits
- ✓ Candidate selection → review → confirm workflow
- ✓ Durable operation receipts with retry contract
- ✓ Batch position tracking and linked events

**5. Concurrent Safety**
- ✓ Revision-aware updates with 409 conflict detection
- ✓ Stale writes rejected with current state returned
- ✓ Safe concurrent updates from multiple tabs/users
- ✓ Operation receipt persistence across restarts

**6. Activity Trail & Audit**
- ✓ Append-only activity log
- ✓ Complete attribution (actor, timestamp, action)
- ✓ Stage changes, panel modifications, scores, notes tracked
- ✓ Assessment version changes recorded with reasons
- ✓ Batch operations linked by ID with positions

**7. Frontend Interface**
